## Spot FX

| requirement    | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| -------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `Spot FX A1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs `SpotFx` proposes both named currency legs in one atomic settlement instruction                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Spot FX A2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs keys every production book and published clearing event by its explicit base and quote currencies                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Spot FX A3`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/currency.rs measures direct-versus-cross inconsistency, but production clears each offered currency independently and no bounded arbitrage order consumes the gap                                                                                                                                              |
| `Spot FX B1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs `SpotFx` nets foreign balances against due schedules and posts only the remaining currency shortfall                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Spot FX B2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs `SpotFx` posts only the foreign-currency balance left after the holder`s own obligations                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Spot FX B3`   | MISSING | no portfolio allocator posts a currency order                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Spot FX B4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs reads each named party's due foreign-currency obligations and posts the residual exposure after its existing foreign balance                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Spot FX B5`   | MISSING | no dealer participant posts a two-sided FX schedule or owns currency inventory                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Spot FX B5.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs `will_quote` is where a dealer would decline the flow it does not want, and no production code names it. Nothing quotes, so nothing is obliged either                                                                                                                                               |
| `Spot FX B6`   | MISSING | no central-bank policy decision posts a bounded FX order                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Spot FX C1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs `Posted`/`clearing` take schedules in rate space; production reads the offered money instrument`s cleared print rather than casting a currency code to an instrument id                                                                                                                             |
| `Spot FX C2`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs clears each offered currency, but cross-pair consistency is neither a clearing constraint nor consumed by bounded arbitrage                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Spot FX C2.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs clears each offered currency on its own book, so consistency across pairs is neither a constraint the solver carries nor a correction applied afterwards. `inconsistency` measures the gap and no production code names it                                                                          |
| `Spot FX C3`   | MISSING | no dealer posts the two-sided schedules from which a bid-offer could emerge                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Spot FX C4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs `clearing` walks the posted supply curve, so added demand reaches a different offered rate rather than a written path                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Spot FX C5`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs uses one clearing rate for both reciprocal settlement legs and journals it, but the rate is not yet a pair-keyed production price used by valuation                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Spot FX C6`   | MISSING | the production world has no pair-keyed history from which zero-flow drift and one-way-flow movement can be measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Spot FX D1`   | MISSING | the settled counterparty positions are real, but no FX dealer participant fills a client and owns the resulting inventory                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Spot FX D2`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs `square` names a counterparty but no production dealer calls it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Spot FX D3`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs can calculate inventory revaluation, but no production dealer carries that inventory                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Spot FX D4`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs expresses a dealer inventory limit, but no production dealer consults it                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Spot FX D5`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs settles a spot exchange only as reciprocal `Receipt::Fx` legs in two currencies, so every trade has two sides and each currency conserves by construction. The VERIFY itself is not taken: `spot_fx.rs position_after` reads the identity and nothing calls it, so dealer and client positions are never summed |
| `Spot FX E1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs refuses self-matches; packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs recognizes an exchange only from two reciprocal `Receipt::Fx` legs in different currencies                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Spot FX E2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/spot_fx.rs (no parity formula, no differential applied to a level, no written path — the FORBID holds by absence)                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Spot FX E3`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/currency.rs `arbitrage` bounds the arbitrageur by its own capital, which is the clause. It is dead code                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Spot FX E4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs lands each reciprocal FX leg as its named money without routing it through the recipient`s home account                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Spot FX F1`   | MISSING | ordinary goods settlement still pays in the buyer`s account money rather than applying one seller-money convention                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Spot FX F1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/ledger.rs requires two reciprocal `Receipt::Fx` legs, so no conversion can occur without the named counterparty leg                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Spot FX F1.b` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs still chooses the buyer`s account instrument, so the settlement money changes with the buyer                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

## Fund Shares

| requirement        | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------ | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Fund Shares A1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/parties.rs and packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs (a fund is a named party with an account and rows)                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Fund Shares A2`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/redeemable.rs `Subscribing` counts shares off `agreed::SUBSCRIPTION` terms. They are agreement terms rather than an instrument holders hold, so A2`s "counted in shares" is a number on a relation, and the `redeemable` module that holds the claim is imported by nothing                                     |
| `Fund Shares A3`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/redeemable.rs `Book::nav` computes assets minus liabilities over shares. Nothing checks that a fund`s equity is zero; the Accounts family is NOT BUILT                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Fund Shares A4`   | PARTIAL | `agreed::MANDATE` carries the lowest grade a pool may hold, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/funds.rs `FundMandates` reads it. Nothing else constrains what a fund buys, and `funds` is imported by nothing                                                                                                                                  |
| `Fund Shares B1`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/redeemable.rs `Book::nav` is a read every time and is stored nowhere. its asset side is one of the three inline copies of units-times-price with a silent fallback to cost                                                                                                                                      |
| `Fund Shares B2`   | PARTIAL | see B1 — a position declared `Carrying::Market` whose line has not printed is now absent from `carrying_value` rather than silently falling back to cost, so whoever asked has to handle it (B2.a). What is still missing is the balance sheet it should reach                                                                                    |
| `Fund Shares B2.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs marks a print stale rather than refreshing it, so a stale input is visible. No subscription or redemption transacts against a NAV built on one, because value reaches no balance sheet and no redemption happens at all                                                                                          |
| `Fund Shares B3`   | MISSING | the only fund mechanism that moves anything is `mechanisms/redeemable.rs Subscribing` (cash in, shares out) and `Winding` (a pool paying its holders). `mechanisms/funds.rs Redeems`, `Redeemed`, `broke_the_buck`, `beats_the_deposit`, `cost_to_those_who_stay`, `shares_reconcile`, `winding_sale`, `mislaid` and `is_wound_up` have no caller |
| `Fund Shares B4`   | MISSING | the only fund mechanism that moves anything is `mechanisms/redeemable.rs Subscribing` (cash in, shares out) and `Winding` (a pool paying its holders). `mechanisms/funds.rs Redeems`, `Redeemed`, `broke_the_buck`, `beats_the_deposit`, `cost_to_those_who_stay`, `shares_reconcile`, `winding_sale`, `mislaid` and `is_wound_up` have no caller |
| `Fund Shares C1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/redeemable.rs `Subscribing` proposes the instruction: cash to the pool, shares to the holder at NAV (one of the ten sites that propose an instruction at all)                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Fund Shares C1.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/redeemable.rs `Subscribing` moves cash to the pool and shares to the holder, and nothing then buys anything with that cash. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/funds.rs `FundMandates` carries what the fund may hold and no purchase is proposed against it                                                     |
| `Fund Shares C2`   | MISSING | `funds.rs Redeems`/`Redeemed` and `redeemable.rs holders_against_the_book` are the redemption and are unreachable. Nothing redeems, so C2.b`s forced-seller channel — "the point" — does not exist                                                                                                                                                |
| `Fund Shares C2.a` | MISSING | no investor can ask for its money back, so no fund has to find cash. packages/kernel-rs/src/module.rs `Meeting` is where a buffer would be drawn on and a sale decided, and no production code names it                                                                                                                                           |
| `Fund Shares C2.b` | MISSING | nothing sells to meet a redemption, so the forced-seller channel carries nothing. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/forced_sale.rs `ForcedSeller` is the participant that would post that sale into a book that must clear, and no fund reaches it                                                                                                |
| `Fund Shares C3`   | PARTIAL | shares rise on a subscription. Nothing takes them back (C2): `redeemable::Meeting` has no caller                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Fund Shares C4`   | MISSING | the only fund mechanism that moves anything is `mechanisms/redeemable.rs Subscribing` (cash in, shares out) and `Winding` (a pool paying its holders). `mechanisms/funds.rs Redeems`, `Redeemed`, `broke_the_buck`, `beats_the_deposit`, `cost_to_those_who_stay`, `shares_reconcile`, `winding_sale`, `mislaid` and `is_wound_up` have no caller |
| `Fund Shares C4.a` | MISSING | no redemption is met by selling, so no cost falls on the holders who stayed. With nothing to bear, a run has no engine and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/funds.rs `Winding` is reached by nothing                                                                                                                                             |
| `Fund Shares C5`   | MISSING | the only fund mechanism that moves anything is `mechanisms/redeemable.rs Subscribing` (cash in, shares out) and `Winding` (a pool paying its holders). `mechanisms/funds.rs Redeems`, `Redeemed`, `broke_the_buck`, `beats_the_deposit`, `cost_to_those_who_stay`, `shares_reconcile`, `winding_sale`, `mislaid` and `is_wound_up` have no caller |

## CDS

| requirement | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ----------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CDS A1`    | MISSING | `Protection` now strikes a typed two-party CDS agreement naming its reference, spread, tenor and settlement currency. It still selects terms without a real CDS order book and does not schedule premium or protection cash flows, so the specified contract is not yet operating                                                                                                                                         |
| `CDS A1.a`  | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/cds.rs `Protection` can name a reference entity, and no default event is defined for it. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs `Missed` is the only thing in the tree that decides a payment was not made, and no contract reads it                                                                                                                                            |
| `CDS A1.b`  | MISSING | no payoff is computed on an event, because no event triggers one. Par minus recovery needs a recovery, and nothing recovers anything: no estate has ever paid a claim                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CDS A1.c`  | MISSING | no running spread clears. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/cds.rs journals a premium from a declared number rather than from a book, and no book in protection is declared anywhere                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CDS A1.d`  | MISSING | no contract carries a tenor, so there is no curve of them and no term structure of credit anywhere in the tree. packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Agreements` carries an `until`, which is where a tenor would sit                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CDS A2`    | MISSING | the `cds` system runs `mechanisms/cds.rs::Protection`, which is wired and can strike a typed CDS agreement from opposing views after an estate recovery exists; it does not schedule premiums, observe later reference events, or propose payouts, so `Contract`, `Series`, `Point`, `net_notional`, `owed_on_event`, `pays_out` and `unfunded_exposure` are unreachable. ## IRS

| requirement | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ----------- | ------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `IRS A1`    | MISSING | `irs` runs `mechanisms/lending.rs Servicing`, the generic pay-what-fell-due mechanism shared with `lending`. `mechanisms::irs` is imported by nothing, so `Swap`, `Net`, `fixes_at` and `pairs_up` are unreachable. There is no fixed leg, no floating leg, no fixing, no netting and no mark |
| `IRS A1.a`  | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs `Fixes` publishes a weekly funding fixing only from a cleared print, so a transacted reference rate is possible. No swap names one, because no swap is struck                                                                                 |
| `IRS A1.b`  | MISSING | no periodic exchange happens. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/lending.rs `Servicing` pays what a schedule says and no swap files a schedule, so nothing nets a fixed leg against a floating one                                                                                             |
| `IRS A1.c`  | MISSING | no fixed rate is cleared. Nothing opens a book in a swap, so the level that would make one worth zero at inception is neither found by a market nor solved for                                                                                                                                |
| `IRS A1.d`  | MISSING | no swap carries a notional at all, so nothing distinguishes one from a loan. packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `AgreementTerms` is where a notional that never moves would be stated                                                                                                           |
| `IRS A2`    | MISSING | `irs` runs `mechanisms/lending.rs Servicing`, the generic pay-what-fell-due mechanism shared with `lending`. `mechanisms::irs` is imported by nothing, so `Swap`, `Net`, `fixes_at` and `pairs_up` are unreachable. There is no fixed leg, no floating leg, no fixing, no netting and no mark |
| `IRS A3`    | MISSING | `irs` runs `mechanisms/lending.rs Servicing`, the generic pay-what-fell-due mechanism shared with `lending`. `mechanisms::irs` is imported by nothing, so `Swap`, `Net`, `fixes_at` and `pairs_up` are unreachable. There is no fixed leg, no floating leg, no fixing, no netting and no mark |
| `IRS A4`    | MET     | a schedule row is in one money, so nothing this system does spans two currencies                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `IRS B1`    | MISSING | `irs` runs `mechanisms/lending.rs Servicing`, the generic pay-what-fell-due mechanism shared with `lending`. `mechanisms::irs` is imported by nothing, so `Swap`, `Net`, `fixes_at` and `pairs_up` are unreachable. There is no fixed leg, no floating leg, no fixing, no netting and no mark |

## Commodities Spot

| requirement             | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ----------------------- | ------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Commodities Spot A1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs `BookDecl` carries the place a goods book is in and `book_here` picks this place's, so a commodity IS a grade at a location in a quantity unit: the line is the grade, the book's region is the location, and packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs gives every line its own `UnitId`. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/commodities.rs `Storing` reads each `Class::Good` line, its named production rows, settled consumption and physical stock against that line's own cleared print where the units are                                                                                          |
| `Commodities Spot A1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs `BookDecl` carries the place a book is in, packages/kernel-rs/src/bin/world_runs.rs opens a goods book in every region and one book everywhere for a line whose identity has no place, and `book_here` is the read that picks this place's. So the same grade in two regions is two books, two runs of prints and two prices. packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs `Marks` is what every value read goes through, so a holder marks at the price where its units are and the gap between two places is what carrying between them costs                                                                   |
| `Commodities Spot A1.b` | MET     | a producer runs the extractive way of the LINE its deposit holds (packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs `Deposit`), so what it makes is what it is standing on. It does not choose a grade: the grade is which line the deposit is of, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `open_to` offers it no way to make a different one from that ground                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Commodities Spot A2`   | MET     | `goods.rs Making` creates output for the named firm that ran the recipe and destroys the named firm’s inputs; `commodities.rs Storing` reads those settled `Create` and `Destroy` legs by line and party                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Commodities Spot A2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `Making` runs an extractive way only for a maker holding a right (packages/kernel-rs/src/registry.rs `rights`) over a tile whose deposit is of that commodity, so which parties can produce at all is a fact of the map. A shortage where there is no deposit is not relieved by any price                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Commodities Spot A3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/commodities.rs `Storing` carries register inventory from one week into the next and proposes the storage payment to the live stockist at that location                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Commodities Spot A4`   | MET     | the register is the sole inventory store by named holder and instrument; `Storing` reads its live total and publishes physical tightness from settled consumption                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Commodities Spot B1`   | MET     | `goods.rs Making` chooses a firm-specific recipe from input, wage and capital-service costs; `commodities.rs Storing` reads the resulting named `Create` rows and derives observed supply at the cleared price from their costs                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Commodities Spot B1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `Making` chooses a recipe from the input, wage and capital-service costs of that firm, so two producers facing different costs offer at different levels. The supply schedule is whatever those offers add up to and no curve is written anywhere                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Commodities Spot B2`   | MET     | `goods.rs decide` is constrained by the firm’s installed capital services and recipes complete only through `InProgress`; no commodity mechanism changes capacity inside the clearing                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Commodities Spot B2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs holds output to the capital services a firm has installed and completes a recipe only through `InProgress`, so nothing can produce more inside the week the book clears. Price is what moves instead                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Commodities Spot B2.b` | MET     | extraction capacity is the plant standing on the ground AND what the ground still holds: packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `open_to` makes a deposit necessary at all, `Reasons::in_the_ground` binds a run to what is left, and `as_it_empties` raises what each unit costs as the seam gives up its cheap ore first. Investment can raise the rate and cannot put a deposit where there is none, nor refill one                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Commodities Spot B3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `interrupted` is the cause and `Finishing` is where it lands: a producer that ceased between starting a run and finishing it, or that no longer holds the plant the run was on, does not make what it started, and the units are said as lost where they would have been made. Nothing multiplies a price, and there is no rate — the cause is something that actually happened to that producer. What a line's capacity IS remains a read of the `Leg::Create` legs that actually settled, so a lost batch shows up as less produced rather than as a second number subtracted from a first |
| `Commodities Spot B4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `GoodsSellers` offers only at the reservation price of the producer, and what does not sell stays in its register holding. Nothing empties an inventory at whatever it fetches                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Commodities Spot C1`   | MET     | `goods.rs Making` destroys the exact inputs a named firm’s selected recipe needs, and `HouseholdBuyers` purchases its declared consumption basket                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Commodities Spot C2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `Making` fixes the input units a recipe wants and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/households.rs `HouseholdBuyers` fixes the basket, both before the book runs. Neither reads the price that book is about to clear at                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Commodities Spot C3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `Stockist` submits funded orders from its own `price_outlook`, competes in the same physical books as everybody else and takes delivery of the same units                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Commodities Spot C4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/clearing.rs `clear` crosses stepped quantity-backed orders from producers, consumers and stockists. No volatility figure exists anywhere, so scarcity moves the price only by changing which posted level is the one that crosses                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Commodities Spot D1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs clears one book at a time and packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs keys what it prints by the market as well as the line, and now every goods book names its region — so a price clears per grade and location, and `book_here` is how a reader names which one it is asking for                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Commodities Spot D2`   | MET     | inventory is the register’s carried state: `GoodsSellers` and `Stockist` can offer only free units they actually hold, so its presence or exhaustion changes the sell schedule seen by clearing                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Commodities Spot D2.a` | MET     | inventory is the carried state of the register rather than a number beside it: packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `GoodsSellers` and `Stockist` can offer only free units they actually hold, and production and consumption are what move that holding. What is left therefore changes the sell schedule the book sees                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Commodities Spot D3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/commodities.rs `Storing` charges every live holder per unit and proposes the payment to the named stockist that owns storage in the location of that holder                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `Commodities Spot D4`   | MISSING | spot now clears from physical orders and `Storing` reads storage cost and the cleared print, but the futures production path is not wired yet, so no traded forward relationship exists                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Commodities Spot D5`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/commodities.rs `CommodityUnits` contributes to the units audit, reconciling settled creation plus prior register stock against settled destruction plus current stock, one physical line at a time                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Commodities Spot E1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `costs` reads cleared input prices through the view of the firm when it chooses a recipe, and those input units are then destroyed with their carried cost entering the output lot                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `Commodities Spot E2`   | MET     | `HouseholdBuyers` buys the registry-declared basket, including energy/commodity lines, with its own cash in the same physical books                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Commodities Spot E3`   | MISSING | `cross_border.rs` preserves party-to-party physical export flows, but commodity terms of trade are not consumed by the currency-fundamentals decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Commodities Spot E4`   | MISSING | the physical price feeds firm input cost and household consumption, but the complete margin-to-inflation-to-policy chain has no wired monetary-policy consumer yet                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Commodities Spot F1`   | MET     | `ledger.rs` pre-checks every `Destroy` against free units, so consumption without settled production or inventory is refused                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Commodities Spot F2`   | MET     | `ledger.rs` refuses a `Destroy` beyond the holding as `ShortOfUnits`; the register cannot become negative through the wire                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Commodities Spot F3`   | MET     | a commodity price is written only by packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs `run_book` out of a completed session, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/commodities.rs only reads the cleared print. No price path is written anywhere                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

## Indices

| requirement    | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| -------------- | ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Indices A1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/registry.rs `index` declares a subject, a scope, a base and a `Weighting` — a RULE, and no list of lines. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs `Index::qualifying` reads the set that rule names out of the instrument store, `admits` says what each subject is an index OF, and `weighs` says what one member carries                                                   |
| `Indices A1.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/registry.rs is the one store of index definitions, so a definition is stable and in one place. It is not public: packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/observer.rs publishes no definition, so nobody outside the kernel can reproduce a level                                                                                                                                           |
| `Indices A2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs `fix` takes only a `Provenance::Cleared` print — a carried or seeded one is refused, which is what "reads cleared prices and nothing else" means                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Indices A3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs (nothing feeds an index level back to a constituent, because no index level is computed at all)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Indices A4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/registry.rs `index_base` is the week each index is based on, declared with the index, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs `PublishedIndices` publishes the level as this week's basket over that week's — with the base beside it, so a reader knows what the level is against. A basket whose base week never priced has a cost and no level, and none is published |
| `Indices B1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/registry.rs `Weighting` states where the weights come from — equal, amount outstanding, or capitalisation — and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs `weighs` reads that basis off the line itself: what is issued of it, and what it is worth at its own print                                                                                                           |
| `Indices B2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs `Index::qualifying` is read at the week it is asked about, so a bond past its maturity is no longer fixed income, a line brought this week is in as soon as it is brought, and `banded` puts a share in the large or small half by rank rather than against a threshold somebody declared. Nobody edits a set                                               |
| `Indices B2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs `chained` walks from the base week forward and each link is the weighted return of what `carried_across` says was in the index in BOTH weeks, at the weights it carried in the earlier one — so a member entering or leaving moves the level by itself not at all, and nothing is stored: the chain is re-walked every time it is published                 |
| `Indices B3`   | MISSING | `mechanisms/benchmarks.rs Index`, `Constituent`, `Base`, `Weighing`, `Fixing`, `divergence`, `on_split`, `squeeze` and `trackers_must_trade` have no caller. Only `fix` is reached, by `mechanisms/benchmarks.rs Fixes`. No index is built, so §22 A, B, C and D1, D2, D4, D5 have nothing behind them                                                                                                      |
| `Indices B4`   | MISSING | `mechanisms/benchmarks.rs Index`, `Constituent`, `Base`, `Weighing`, `Fixing`, `divergence`, `on_split`, `squeeze` and `trackers_must_trade` have no caller. Only `fix` is reached, by `mechanisms/benchmarks.rs Fixes`. No index is built, so §22 A, B, C and D1, D2, D4, D5 have nothing behind them                                                                                                      |
| `Indices C1`   | MISSING | `mechanisms/benchmarks.rs Index`, `Constituent`, `Base`, `Weighing`, `Fixing`, `divergence`, `on_split`, `squeeze` and `trackers_must_trade` have no caller. Only `fix` is reached, by `mechanisms/benchmarks.rs Fixes`. No index is built, so §22 A, B, C and D1, D2, D4, D5 have nothing behind them                                                                                                      |
| `Indices C2`   | MISSING | `mechanisms/benchmarks.rs Index`, `Constituent`, `Base`, `Weighing`, `Fixing`, `divergence`, `on_split`, `squeeze` and `trackers_must_trade` have no caller. Only `fix` is reached, by `mechanisms/benchmarks.rs Fixes`. No index is built, so §22 A, B, C and D1, D2, D4, D5 have nothing behind them                                                                                                      |
| `Indices C2.a` | MISSING | `mechanisms/benchmarks.rs Index`, `Constituent`, `Base`, `Weighing`, `Fixing`, `divergence`, `on_split`, `squeeze` and `trackers_must_trade` have no caller. Only `fix` is reached, by `mechanisms/benchmarks.rs Fixes`. No index is built, so §22 A, B, C and D1, D2, D4, D5 have nothing behind them                                                                                                      |
| `Indices C3`   | MISSING | `mechanisms/benchmarks.rs Index`, `Constituent`, `Base`, `Weighing`, `Fixing`, `divergence`, `on_split`, `squeeze` and `trackers_must_trade` have no caller. Only `fix` is reached, by `mechanisms/benchmarks.rs Fixes`. No index is built, so §22 A, B, C and D1, D2, D4, D5 have nothing behind them                                                                                                      |
| `Indices C4`   | MISSING | `mechanisms/benchmarks.rs Index`, `Constituent`, `Base`, `Weighing`, `Fixing`, `divergence`, `on_split`, `squeeze` and `trackers_must_trade` have no caller. Only `fix` is reached, by `mechanisms/benchmarks.rs Fixes`. No index is built, so §22 A, B, C and D1, D2, D4, D5 have nothing behind them                                                                                                      |
| `Indices D1`   | MET     | the assembled world declares all-, small- and large-cap equity definitions per currency and globally, and `PublishedIndices` computes them from same-week constituent prints                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Indices D2`   | MET     | the assembled world declares separate investment-grade and high-yield fixed-bond and CDS definitions plus a high-yield tradable-term-loan definition per currency; bank loans are not constituents                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Indices D3`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs `Fixes` publishes a weekly funding fixing from a cleared print and refuses anything else. one book of 1,546 prints per period, so there is almost never a fixing to publish                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Indices D3.a` | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs `Fixes` publishes a weekly funding fixing from a cleared print and refuses to publish from anything else, so a fixing is a read of transactions when there is one. No floating coupon fixes on it, because no instrument carries one                                                                                                                        |
| `Indices D3.b` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs `fix` returns `None` for `Provenance::Carried` and `Provenance::Seeded`, so a posted rate can never be a benchmark                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Indices D4`   | MET     | the assembled world declares separate consumer and producer index definitions and `PublishedIndices` computes each definition independently                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Indices D4.a` | MET     | the assembled world declares a consumer and a producer index as separate definitions and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs `PublishedIndices` computes each from its own constituents, so input prices and output prices move as two readings and the gap between them survives                                                                                                               |
| `Indices D5`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/registry.rs is the one index-definition store and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs is the one level reader                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Indices D5.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs stores no level and recomputes one from the definition each period, so nothing invents a history. There is no history either: the opening state is arbitrary, so every covariance measured against it is measured against that                                                                                                                              |
| `Indices E1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/registry.rs refuses an empty constituent definition                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Indices E2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/benchmarks.rs recomputes observations from registry definitions and price prints; no index level store exists                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Indices E3`   | MISSING | `mechanisms/benchmarks.rs Index`, `Constituent`, `Base`, `Weighing`, `Fixing`, `divergence`, `on_split`, `squeeze` and `trackers_must_trade` have no caller. Only `fix` is reached, by `mechanisms/benchmarks.rs Fixes`. No index is built, so §22 A, B, C and D1, D2, D4, D5 have nothing behind them                                                                                                      |

| `Banks Lending A1`   | PARTIAL | `agreed::COMMITMENT` is a named lender to a named borrower and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/lending.rs `Rows` is a book of loan rows. Nothing originates one, and the origination IS the wiring                                                                                          |
| `Banks Lending A1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs keeps a loan as an `agreed::COMMITTED_CREDIT` between two named parties rather than as an instrument in the register, so it has no issue, no holders and no book. Nothing can price or transfer it                                                           |
| `Banks Lending A1.b` | PARTIAL | a loan sits in `Agreements` and never in the register, so it is not marked with the instruments that are. ## Banks Funding

| requirement          | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| -------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Banks Funding A1`   | MISSING | `BankFunding` now proposes bounded sales and a collateralised facility draw, but `Line`, `after_outflow`, `buffer_wanted`, `balances`, `net_interest_margin` and `transformation` still have no production caller. Deposit classes and run behaviour remain unwired |
| `Banks Funding A1.a` | MISSING | no deposit base is split by who the depositor is, so nothing is retail. packages/kernel-rs/src/parties.rs carries the integer weight that would make an insured amount exact for a cell, and no insurance limit is declared for it to multiply                      |
| `Banks Funding A1.b` | MISSING | a firm holds an account at the bank it was admitted with and nothing marks that funding as operational, so no balance is stickier than another for a reason                                                                                                         |
| `Banks Funding A1.c` | MISSING | no depositor is rate-sensitive, because no bank posts a deposit rate for one to respond to. Every balance stays where it is whatever happens                                                                                                                        |

| -------------------- | ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Banks Capital A1`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `equity` is a residual and is never a stored pot, and `booked_equity` reads every position under the treatment its holder declared. A bank that declared `Carrying::Cost` is not moved by a mark, which is the mechanism XI-6 describes rather than a gap; the loss reaches it on the day it sells, and nothing forces that sale yet    |
| `Banks Capital A1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `equity` is computed at every read and nothing withdraws from it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Banks Capital A2`   | MISSING | `BankCapital` reads a bank position against two declared ratios and journals. It proposes nothing, and `mechanisms/bank_capital.rs Absorbed`, `Binding`, `Conservation`, `absorb`, `hole`, `trigger` and `no_creditor_worse_off` have no caller. Nothing recapitalises, nothing is resolved, nothing is bailed in                                                             |
| `Banks Capital A2.a` | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/estate.rs `Rank::Equity` is last in the waterfall, so equity is what a loss reaches first. It absorbs nothing in practice, because no loss is booked against a bank's equity outside a wind-up                                                                                                                                              |
| `Banks Capital A2.b` | MISSING | no instrument states a rank, so no subordinated layer exists between equity and senior paper. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/estate.rs `Rank` has the ordering and nothing issues into it                                                                                                                                                                                  |

## Private Equity

| requirement           | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| --------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Private Equity A1`   | MISSING | `private_equity` runs `mechanisms/private_equity.rs::Calling`, which is wired and schedules capital calls under typed commitments. Its buyout, marking, recapitalisation and exit helpers are not called by `Calling::run`; no buyout happens and nothing exits                                                                                                                       |
| `Private Equity A2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/private_equity.rs `Calling` writes the call as an obligation the investor owes the fund by name, falling due at the next period`s open — real money out of a named investor`s balance, **on a date it cannot refuse**, and not in the week the call was made (Money G1.c)                                                                           |
| `Private Equity A2.a` | MISSING | `private_equity` runs `mechanisms/private_equity.rs::Calling`, which is wired and schedules capital calls under typed commitments. Its buyout, marking, recapitalisation and exit helpers are not called by `Calling::run`; no buyout happens and nothing exits                                                                                                                       |
| `Private Equity A2.b` | PARTIAL | the call falls due like any other payment and the wire refuses or queues it where the investor is short, so it is not bounded by spare cash. What is missing is the other half: an investor that does not pay does not DEFAULT on it — `mechanisms/loss.rs` crosses a standing on a LINE and a bilateral obligation has none, so the row runs out of periods and no fund learns of it |
| `Private Equity A3`   | MISSING | `private_equity` runs `mechanisms/private_equity.rs::Calling`, which is wired and schedules capital calls under typed commitments.
