## Expectations

| requirement         | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------- | ------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Expectations A1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Outlooks` keys an outlook by party and by subject and `form` refuses one with no holder, while packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/expectations.rs `Forming` builds the things a party acts on: what it sells for, what it keeps earning, what credit costs, how much it sells and whether a name pays it back                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Expectations A2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/expectations.rs `Forming` forms each party`s outlook from the prints on the lines IT holds and what IT delivered, and from nothing else                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `Expectations A2.a` | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/observer.rs journals a count and publishes no aggregate, so there is no statistic for a party to observe. Nothing lags, nothing revises, and no outlook has a published number among its observations                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Expectations A2.b` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Outlooks` is keyed by party; there is no global expectation anywhere                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `Expectations A3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/module.rs `price_outlook` and `outlook` answer for the asking party alone, so two parties with different histories read different numbers into the same book and the two sides of it are their disagreement                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Expectations A4`   | MET     | `Forming` corrects last period`s outlook toward what was observed. Nothing runs the world forward                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Expectations A5`   | MISSING | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Outlooks` carries a level and no unit or horizon; `about::` names the subject and not the periodicity                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Expectations B1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/parties.rs `with_seed_and_memory` draws one reproducible memory horizon per party at admission, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/expectations.rs `Forming` corrects each outlook towards what happened at the speed of that party                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Expectations B1.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/parties.rs `with_seed_and_memory` gives each party one memory drawn at admission from a declared range, so members of one sector weigh different numbers of their own periods. It is the only preference the system takes and nothing redraws it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Expectations B1.b` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `observe` is the one writer of an outlook and it takes an observation, so nothing else moves one. `confidence` is derived from the recorded surprises rather than held, and no anchor, target or sentiment figure is stored anywhere                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `Expectations B2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Outlooks::observe` records party, subject, period, expected and observed before adapting the level                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Expectations B2.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `forecast_errors` returns the surprises as dated rows readable by party and by subject, so an outlook that moved can be put beside the surprise behind it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Expectations B3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `confidence` derives the width from each party`s own recent absolute forecast errors; dealing consumes it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `Expectations B4`   | MET     | `Forming` reads the last outlook and this period`s prints, never the period`s own result of the decision it feeds                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Expectations B5`   | PARTIAL | the mechanism the VERIFY needs is there: packages/kernel-rs/src/parties.rs disperses the memory horizon across parties and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/expectations.rs `Forming` corrects at the speed of each one, so lags differ by party by construction. The measurement against a turning point has not been taken                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Expectations C1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/households.rs `HouseholdBuyers` bids from the `price_outlook` of the cell itself, its spendable cash and the buffer it keeps, so a cell expecting a fall bids lower out of the same money                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Expectations C2`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `Making` reads the firm`s own `HOW_MUCH_IT_SELLS`for the production decision, packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/capital_programme.rs`Building` reads it for the investment one and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/employment.rs reads it for hiring — a firm with no view does not act on somebody else`s. It is one outlook doing all three: the price it expects to sell at is declared and nothing reads it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Expectations C2.a` | MISSING | the clause asks for an outlook a party holds ABOUT another party, published and weighable. `repayment_of` and `loss_given_failure_of` key exactly that, and nothing forms either: no bank estimates a company's coming report and no such estimate is published                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Expectations C3`   | MET     | every participant builds its reservation from `price_outlook` and its own position, and packages/kernel-rs/src/session.rs `run_book` writes the print only after the book has cleared. The view that meets a book was therefore formed before that book produced anything                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `Expectations C4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `repayment_of` keys a lender outlook to the named borrower and `Forming` moves it from what that lender saw of that name, while packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/dealing.rs `Dealers` quotes around the `price_outlook` it holds on the line it makes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Expectations C5`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs reads its own `price_outlook` on the line it is about to auction, so the public sector gets no better forecast than anybody else. The central-bank half is absent: no such party exists to hold an outlook or to read one                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Expectations C6`   | MISSING | see C1, and Polity: no cell votes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Expectations D1`   | MET     | `Forming` reads only the party`s own rows and the public prints on them                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Expectations D2`   | MET     | `Forming` forms per party, cells included, and nothing averages across them                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Expectations D3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `observe` scores every outlook against what actually arrived and keeps the surprise, and `forecast_errors` hands those scores back. An outlook nothing scores cannot exist, because scoring is the only thing that moves one                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Expectations D4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `about` admits what a firm earns and what it sells, a price on a named line, what credit costs, what a house is worth, and whether a named borrower pays back — and nothing else. No outlook can be about a mood, an index or a survey, so no aggregate of outlooks is available to be a cause                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Expectations E1`   | MISSING | a Part XII measurement; there is no dispersion to read while every reservation is the same constant                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Expectations E2`   | MISSING | outlooks are formed correctly by `mechanisms/expectations.rs Forming` and NOTHING READS THEM — `grep outlooks() systems.rs` is empty. `mechanisms::expectations` is imported by nothing                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Expectations E3`   | MISSING | outlooks are formed correctly by `mechanisms/expectations.rs Forming` and NOTHING READS THEM — `grep outlooks() systems.rs` is empty. `mechanisms::expectations` is imported by nothing                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Expectations E4`   | MISSING | outlooks are formed correctly by `mechanisms/expectations.rs Forming` and NOTHING READS THEM — `grep outlooks() systems.rs` is empty. `mechanisms::expectations` is imported by nothing                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Expectations F1`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/module.rs `Valuer` is the seam: the kernel asks the system that owns a family what a thing of it is worth. Two families answer — a claim out of its dated payments in packages/kernel-rs/src/module.rs `fair_value`, and a plant out of what it can produce in packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/capital_programme.rs `worth_per_unit`. A good answers too, in packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `worth_in_use` — what the output it goes into fetches, less everything else that unit of output takes, over what it takes of this one. A share, a company and a dwelling have no value yet                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Expectations F1.a` | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/module.rs `values` falls through a party's own outlook, then the registered valuer for that line's class, then paper comparable to what it has a view of — so a claim, a plant or a good with no print still has a bid — a good to whoever can work it, which is the party a primary goods book needs a side from. A good to whoever only consumes it has none, and a share, a company or a dwelling has none at all                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Expectations F1.b` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/module.rs `fair_value` reads only this party's own `WHAT_CREDIT_COSTS` outlook and its own `standing::OWN_VIEW` of the name, plus the contract on the schedule. There is no term in it that another party supplied                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `Expectations F1.c` | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/module.rs `fair_value` answers per unit of the line, divided by what `outstanding_of` says is out — so it is comparable with the price the book quotes. It carries no currency beside it: the line's `ccy_of` says what money it is in and the value does not                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `Expectations F2`   | PARTIAL | every participant's limit is now its OWN value: what a buyer will pay up to and what a seller will come down to, so the gap between that level and what the book crosses at is the party's own surplus. What is short is the other half of F2 — a party that declines for want of a gap, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/capital_programme.rs `worth_doing` is the only test of a return against a hurdle in the tree                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Expectations F2.a` | PARTIAL | no rule in the tree assigns a side by party kind: packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/funds.rs, packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/hedge_funds.rs and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/insurers.rs each read their own level and post from it. What is short is the comparison that would make the side follow from the level rather than from which order the participant happens to build                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `Expectations F2.b` | MISSING | a limit at a party's own value means it trades at its value and gains nothing rather than standing aside. Nothing in the tree declines for want of a gap: packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/dealing.rs quotes both sides every week it has a level and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/money_market.rs bids whenever it has spare cash                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Expectations F3`   | PARTIAL | packages/kernel-rs/src/module.rs `Valuer` dispatches on the line's class and the answer is the family's own: packages/kernel-rs/src/module.rs `worth_of` takes a claim's dated payment, a chance of being paid and a required return; packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/capital_programme.rs `worth_per_unit` takes a plant's capacity, upkeep and life against what its output earns; packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `worth_in_use` takes the recipe's own coefficients, its yield and how long it takes to run. Nothing shared sits between them. The other three families have no terms written                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Expectations F4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/module.rs `fair_value` reads the deciding party's own outlook and its own standing view, and packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Outlooks` keys every outlook by party — so two parties agree on a value only when their histories agree. Nothing in the tree computes a value once and hands it to everybody                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Expectations F5`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/prices.rs `write` is settlement's and clearing's alone and takes a `Print` with its provenance, and packages/kernel-rs/src/instruments.rs `carrying_value` marks a market-carried position from a print or answers that it has none. No value reaches either, and packages/kernel-rs/src/module.rs `fair_value` is read by participants only                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `Expectations F6`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/module.rs `fair_value` refuses where the party holds no `WHAT_CREDIT_COSTS` outlook of its own rather than reading a parameter, and packages/kernel-rs/src/params.rs declares no level any party values at. Nothing aggregates other parties' values                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `Expectations F7`   | MISSING | a Part XII measurement, and it needs books that clear: packages/kernel-rs/src/bin/world_runs.rs reports 4,497 books open in week 1 and none of them clearing, so there is no depth to read dispersion against                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Expectations F8`   | MET     | no participant posts at a print. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/forced_sale.rs posts no limit at all, because XI-2 sells at whatever the market gives it and a seller that can refuse is not forced; packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/hedge_funds.rs, packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/capital_programme.rs, packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs, packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/funds.rs, packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/households.rs, packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/insurers.rs, packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/sovereign.rs and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/treasury.rs each take `values`, which is the party's own outlook, then paper it has a view of, then what the contract is worth to it. The one remaining read of a print is packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/dealing.rs, which uses it as the disagreement its width comes from and not as its level |

## Geography

| requirement    | status  | where / why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| -------------- | ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `Geography A1` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/assembly.rs `World` holds one `geography`, generated in `World::new` from the run's declared grid, and packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs `generate` builds a finite `Vec<Tile>` where each tile's `TileId` is its row and its `Coordinate` is on the one projection `Provenance` names                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Geography A2` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs `Surface` is land or water and has no third value, so an unknown surface is unconstructible. `generate` gives every tile one of the two by comparing its elevation with the declared sea level                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `Geography A3` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs `neighbors` is the one adjacency read, derived from the grid, and `TopologyIsReciprocal` measures every week that a tile is its neighbour's neighbour both ways. Nothing else in the tree reconstructs adjacency                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Geography A4` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs carries `Kilometres`, `SquareKilometres` and `Metres` as their own types on every coordinate, area and elevation, and `TileId` is a row number with no arithmetic on it — `distance` is reached through the coordinates, never through the id                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `Geography A5` | PARTIAL | terrain constrains a site: packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs `site` refuses water and `stand` needs land the region actually holds. It constrains no route, because no route is built. packages/kernel-rs/src/assembly.rs `every_store_is_declared` refuses a second map that nobody declared                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Geography B1` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs `distance` derives a physical length in kilometres from the coordinates `generate` laid down and the tile side `GenerationShape` states, and `DistanceIsPhysical` takes that read every week                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

| ---------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `Geography I1`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs `Deposit` names the tile it is under and the commodity LINE it holds, and `Held` says whether that quantity is a declared finite amount or unbounded — packages/kernel-rs/src/bin/world_runs.rs states a finite one. The grade is which line it is, per I1.a, not a second field beside it — so the same commodity at two grades is two deposits of two things. `deposit_at` is the read, and it answers for one tile and one thing: a tile holds one deposit of a thing or none                                                                                               |
| `Geography I1.a` | MISSING | nothing in the tree holds what the ground holds: a tile carries a coordinate, an area, an elevation and a surface, and no deposit, grade or quantity of anything, so two grades of one commodity cannot be two things because neither is anything                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `Geography I2`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `open_to` drops an extractive way wherever the maker is not standing on a deposit of what that way makes, and `Making` asks `deposit_at` through a right the maker actually holds. A region with no deposit of a thing produces none of it at any price, and what a place can make is a fact about the place                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `Geography I3`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs `Deposit` carries what a seam OPENED with and what has left it since, and `extract` — settlement's own writer — is the only thing that moves the second. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `Making` takes an extractive run's starts out of the tile it is standing on, so what is extracted leaves the ground and does not come back. A run cannot take more than is there: `extract` refuses it at the site, which is arithmetic impossibility rather than a limit anybody set. An unbounded seam answers `left` with nothing, because it has nothing to run out of |
| `Geography I3.a` | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs `as_it_empties` is what a seam has already given up against what it opened with, and packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs scales the extractive way by it beside crowding — so an extractor's labour and capital per unit RISE with what it has already taken, and nobody wrote a cost path. It is a read of the ground's own state. An unbounded seam returns 1.0 and holds its grade                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `Geography I4`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/registry.rs `rights` says which tile and which commodity each right line is over, and the right itself is an ordinary one-unit holding in packages/kernel-rs/src/register.rs — granted by crediting it, and sold or lost like any other unit. packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `Making` reads both and will not run an extractive way for a maker holding no right over ground that holds the deposit                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Geography I5`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs `left` is a read of what the seam opened with less what has gone, so extracted plus what is left IS what it opened with and the identity cannot fail by arithmetic. `extract` refuses the one way it could — taking more than is there — and `GroundBalances` guards the other: a seam whose taken total has passed what it held or fallen back below nothing is reported per tile and line, because a FORBID that holds breaks silently                                                                                                                                       |
| `Geography I6`   | MET     | packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/goods.rs `open_to` refuses extraction where the ground holds no deposit, and `deposit_at` in packages/kernel-rs/src/geography.rs answers per TILE — so a commodity under some tiles and not others is a world whose places differ in what they can make, which is the half of the FORBID the map decides. A deposit needs a land tile to be under, and no finite deposit refills: `taken` only ever rises and `extract` is its one writer                                                                                                                                        |
